# D3 lifetime route: the native handle-status query is eliminated and a live native handle stops by name (candidate; 1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; a focused needed-only extraction under Task 51 / PR 111, not whole-D3 closure; after 1d644b251)

WHAT CHANGED (TL needed-only decision). The hosted campaign no longer asks the legacy sound system whether a native playback handle is playing. `se_updt.c` loses `host_handle_playing` and its compiled call of `get_system_sound_effect_playing`, and both callers of `campaign_sound_lifetime_update` (server and client) now hand over only whether a handle is held (`ss_hostenv.[ch]`: the `void *handle` and the callback parameter become `int has_handle`; the host request and reply schema, the ABI and every Rust-side protocol check are unchanged, and `sound_have_playing` and `sound_playing` are simply never set). If the host asks for the status at the valid server expiry point (`reply.sound_need_playing` exactly TRUE, in the server routine of a valid effect that holds a handle) the engine stops with `[env-sound:unsupported_handle_status]` BEFORE any native getter or device query, any lifetime write, kill, unlink or destruction; the status is never defaulted to FALSE or taken from the reply. A request the protocol does not allow is still the existing protocol error, and the second-request error is gone because nothing is answered. This is an explicitly UNSUPPORTED boundary, not playback, a no-op or a drop. Unchanged: the original game's `#else` (it still calls the getter), the no-handle and default behaviour, the host's lifetime arithmetic, the Core child, kill, unlink, network and order bookkeeping, and every other sound route. The headless route creates no native handle (the original constructor and the backend are false; the accepted metadata and backend evidence is reused), so nothing reaches the boundary today. Not claimed removed: the other getters (`se_snd.c`, `en_snd.c`), `destroy_system_sound_effect`, the file loaders, the device ownership and every other legacy sound call; those stay OPEN for their own needed-only disposition and no global dormancy follows.

NEW CANDIDATE WITNESS (small, separate). `eech-campaign/crates/eech-world/lua/env-sound-handle-guard.lua` and `tests/env_sound_handle_guard.rs` (two tests): the driver runs ONE existing fixture case, 21 or 22 (`eech_env_fixture.c env_snd_handle_case`, byte-exact 4238a35e6, unchanged), through the real public host Lua `env_case`, and accepts exactly ONE outcome, the engine's own fatal naming `[env-sound:unsupported_handle_status]`. A normal report (the original native), a refusal of the case, any other stop, a repeated name, a missing method, a bad case and every output failure fail. After the stop the engine is poisoned and the driver makes NO further engine call: no census, no restoration, no receiver check; the static test-owned record stays valid through the fatal, the fixture cleanup does not run, and the driver never claims the campaign continued. An expected stop is an `expected_refusal` row with the process exiting 0 (the outer diagnostic only). The whole fatal text is kept in the PRIVATE `<out>.fatal.raw` (written before it is judged); the JSON holds only the named boundary and fixed words (another stop's bracketed tag only, never its text). Both evidence files are created exclusively before boot, every write and flush is checked, and BOTH are closed (the private raw first) with every failure reported and a failed close failing the run. The ORIGINAL `lua/env-sound-handle.lua` and `tests/env_sound_handle.rs` are byte-exact 1d644b251, and the older cases, oracles and catalogue 164 are untouched. The C fixture's own comments still describe the removed callback (its bytes were kept exact), and the Rust host protocol still carries the unused status fields.

ORIGINAL CHARACTERIZATION (independently closed; reported by Root, not reproduced by the author). Canonical actual report `independent-environment-handle-status-original-actual-native4238-driver1d-20261101.json` (SHA256 `fd167d91…0139c`, findings [], `finite_original_handle_status_characterization_closed=true`), Root audit `2116ab00…9711`: the exact native 4238 with the EXPLICIT external Lua 1d (`3f8cba…142d`), no relabel; the first two controls native/controllers 0, 4 full 1179-file input checks 0, 71 payloads and the driver unchanged; both made 3 calls with 6 probe records and 4 states (CREATE, ENTER 1/2/3, IDLE 3, KILL 3), the handle released by the original only, and the cleanup read after zeroing; case 22's marker went 1 to 0 BEFORE the fixture cleanup (a MEASURED getter write), case 21 is inferred only; all 18 other complete raw lines are exact and the 4 declared case-identity and initial-marker lines differ (the full raw is FALSE); the handle is a TEST-OWNED typed static-array record with a NULL buffer, not an original constructor product, a backend, a playing buffer or audio; no RNG fingerprint, full caller floating point, shipping or Tacview claim. The final original source and guard review `b571…` cleared the three prior witness issues; Linux and Windows 6 guards each and fmt/strict 0, with the genuine old 8bb false-accept RED and the old 4238 close REDs retained; all 9 CI jobs of 1d644b251 are SUCCESS per Root's live gh (the receipt follows). The original runs are not repeated for handover.

# D3 handle-status ORIGINAL characterization: formatting, evidence-file closure, diagnostics, state-flag joins and causal contradictions corrected (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; driver and test only; after 4238a35e6)

Actual gate (Root's, reported; not reproduced): at 4238a35e6 `cargo fmt --all --check` FAILED in `tests/env_sound_handle.rs` BEFORE any compile, so no red, green or strict test ran (`handle-status-correction-linux-redgreen-4238a35e6-20261101/transport.log`). Formatter: no local `rustfmt` or `cargo` exists on this machine; the pinned repo formatter image that already existed was run once over a private copy of the tree (offline, source mounted read-only, nothing installed): exit 0, only the test file differed, and a second pass over the formatted file changed nothing. Run by the author: that formatter and `git diff --check` only; no test, compile, native run or campaign.

Corrections (same reviewer residual batch, one commit): (1) the evidence-file list was shadowed by the creation record, so the final close loop closed neither file and ignored errors; the list is now `opened` and the creation record `creation`, and the driver closes BOTH files (the raw sidecar first, so a failure to close it can still be written to the JSON as `output_failure`), attempts every close, reports every failure and then fails closed: a `result` row is not enough without a succeeding process. (2) A parsed map holds only the report's own keys, so `r.raw` and `created.raw` concatenated a nil before `hard()` and lost the `witness_failure` row; the raw text now lives in a separate weak table read by `raw_of`, the grammar stays closed, and every changed diagnostic path is exercised by a damage whose `witness_failure` row must carry its reason. (3) A later live state's validity, update-list membership, handle, looping and sub type are now joined to the probe record of the same instant (the entry of update u to the state after call u-1, a playing expiry of update u to the state after call u), and every stop has the helper's own reason: each state but the last is a live member of the update list, and fewer than three calls require a dead entity or one that left the update list; a live member entity with two calls and one skipped is refused, while an early kill, a last-call kill, an entity alive with no expiry and a live entity that left the update list stay accepted. No lifetime is computed and no kill is forced. (4) Causal contradictions are failures: a stale marker that survives an expiry with a handle, a marker cleared with no expiry that reached the getter, and a flag that became set during the calls each write a `getter_diagnostic` row and then fail with a `witness_failure` and no result, with the private raw kept; an UNCHANGED stale marker with no expiry stays an accepted observation and is not getter coverage, and a marker measured set then clear remains the only measured getter evidence, apart from the source-backed inference.

Guards (six tests in the same file; the selector `EECH_SOUND_HANDLE_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST` is unchanged: `var_os`, `PathBuf`, default only when absent, unreadable or non-Unicode fails, no environment mutation): the coherent C-shaped positives are first (the contradiction shapes are NOT among them), then 61 finite damaged reports, then the contradiction test and the close test with an injected close failure on either file after a real close (the wrapper records every close the driver makes). # D3 handle-status ORIGINAL characterization: the two grouped source findings corrected (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; characterization only; the C fixture readback moved, so the native needs an ordinary rebuild; after 8bb0fae0f)

The same independent source report `independent-environment-handle-status-original-source-8bb0fae0f-20261101.json` (SHA256 `5bd8931b…4485`) found exactly two grouped P2s, ENV-HANDLE-ORIGINAL-1 and -2, and no separate C memory-safety blocker. Both are corrected together here; nothing else changed: no production line, no getter, destructor or physics, no host ABI or schema, no existing case, oracle, driver or baseline, and the original behaviour is still only characterized.

GROUP 1 (protocol joins, `lua/env-sound-handle.lua`, `tests/env_sound_handle.rs`). The driver now joins what the C probe actually serializes: the fixed-capacity report (capacity 80, ONE tracked creation, no dropped or untracked record, no open episode, no natural frame, tracking switched off); the ENTER records against the calls made (update numbers 1 to calls in order, the idle expiry and the kill at the update that is running, the kill the last record); the entity's slot, type, sub type, looping, sample sequence and controlled descriptor (rate 4, size 20, the facts' original rate), the delta word and the metadata and position words against the creation record; the state words against the probe words of the same instant (the creation is the first state, the entry of update u the state after call u-1, a playing expiry of update u the state after call u); the controlled descriptor and delta input rows against the facts; and the final alive or dead state against the cleanup, the kill record and the handle readbacks. It computes no lifetime and forces no kill at any step: an early kill, a kill at the last call and an entity that stays alive with or without an expiry are all legitimate observations. The getter evidence is labelled: that the host's question reached the real getter at an expiry is INFERRED from the source and the probe's expiry-with-handle records, and only a stale `playing` marker that reads set before the calls and clear after them is a MEASUREMENT that the getter wrote the flag; a marker preserved after an expiry with a handle is reported as `marker_preserved_contradicts_the_inferred_getter_call`, never as a getter reached. The scripted positives now follow the C serializer: a KILLED record carries the freed slot only (zero raw fields, no sample, the freed type) and the dead state shows zero words. The same four tests carry the coherent C-shaped positives first (kills at call 1, 2 and 3, alive with and without an expiry, a playing expiry, three marker shapes) and then 52 finite damaged reports (every counter, update and episode label, slot, state-word and metadata overlap, controlled descriptor, delta and sequence field, kill-record raw datum, alive-or-dead and cleanup story, marker and readback damage), each of which must change the text, fail with its reason, keep the raw and produce no result. The test-only selector `EECH_SOUND_HANDLE_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST` reads a path with `var_os` and `PathBuf`: only an absent variable selects the committed driver, an unreadable, empty, directory or non-Unicode value fails and never falls back, and the selection is a pure function (no test mutates the environment).

GROUP 2 (C fixture and prose truth). The handle's final readback (`used_final`, `playing_final`, `buffer_null_final`) is now taken AFTER the record is zeroed, so it is the actual final record; who released the handle (the original's kill or the fixture) is still decided from the snapshot taken right after the calls, before the fixture touched anything, and the row grammar is unchanged. The comments, the driver header and this entry now say: the normal-return cleanup (detach, release, zero, descriptor and delta restored, tracking turned OFF, which is a reset to off and not a restore of a previous state) is separate from a fatal unwind, which poisons the engine and ends the isolated process with nothing restored or inferred; the PRIVATE raw report contains the probe's entity slot and type words and the sound entity's `px_w`, `py_w` and `pz_w` position words (the reused tracking rows), which need a precise safe projection before anything is published to Git, and no pointer is in it (the earlier claim that no index or coordinate is printed was wrong); and the JSON file holds summaries (control, unsupported, derived, result and failure rows) while the complete report is the raw sidecar `<out>.case.raw`, not one JSON row per report row. Actuals of a run must require the process success as well as the `result` row, because the final close can fail after it.

FOR ROOT (nothing run by the author; no actual exit is claimed; rustfmt was NOT run in this pass, by instruction, so the format gate is Root's). Compile the new guards once, then ONE meaningful RED against the frozen 8bb0fae0f driver: `EECH_SOUND_HANDLE_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST=D:/EECH-Migration-Evidence/hs-8bb0fae0f/eech-campaign/crates/eech-world/lua/env-sound-handle.lua cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_sound_handle a_report_that_is_incomplete_contradictory_or_not_the_case_asked_for_is_a_failure_of_the_witness`. Expected by reading, not measured: that driver accepts the eight coherent C-shaped positives of the test (its grammar and chain rules accept a zero-field kill record, its cleanup row keys are the same), and then FAILS at the first damaged report with "the damaged report was ACCEPTED" (the first line, `creation count of the probe changed`, because it never reconciled the probe's totals, the update numbers, the slot or the metadata); it does not fail by a setup or schema mismatch. If that driver's own handling of the C-shaped killed record proves to be a defect, record it separately and use the alive or early-kill positives for the false-accept counter case. Then the GREEN with the variable unset: `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_sound_handle` (all four tests, including the getter-label test and the selector guard). Because the C fixture's final readback moved, the native must be rebuilt ordinarily; no Lua-only equivalence or relabelling of 8bb is claimed. The two fresh verified retail roots `D:/EECH-Migration-Evidence/sound-handle-original-roots-20261101-1345/{idle,stale}` (prep 30417 collected 0) are UNUSED; the source findings block their native use until this correction is accepted. Still UNMEASURED: every native result (the getter's effect, the host's answer, the kill, the real destroy, the cleanup), a playing buffer (needs a device; not manufactured), the original constructor's handle, the unlink-path destroy, the other getter callers, network and audio.

# D3 sound handle status at a server expiry: the ORIGINAL buffer-absent idle handle characterization prepared (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; CHARACTERISATION ONLY; a focused ORIGINAL control under Task 51 / PR 111, not whole-D3 closure; after f4c661fc4)

WHAT THIS ADDS. The one next missing physical coupling of the sound lifetime route: `se_updt.c update_server` (hosted block) gives a held handle to `campaign_sound_lifetime_update`; for an expired effect the HOST asks whether the handle is playing and the campaign answers with `host_handle_playing`, which is the REAL compiled `modules/sound/soundman.c get_system_sound_effect_playing` (it clears `effect->playing` and returns FALSE when `effect->sound_buffer` is NULL, and only otherwise asks the DirectSound buffer); a kill then reaches `se_dstry.c` and the REAL `destroy_system_sound_effect` (ASSERT used; release the buffer if any; clear `used`). The closed no-handle controls (cases 4 to 14) never reach that branch and printed it as unsupported. New fixture cases in group 14 (`csrc/eech_env_fixture.c env_snd_handle_case`, observer build only, explicit, PROCESS-ISOLATED): `21 handle_idle_buffer_absent_server_expiry` (the handle's `playing` starts 0) and `22 handle_idle_buffer_absent_stale_playing_server_expiry` (it starts 1: a SENSITIVITY MARKER, because the real getter writes the flag). Each builds ONE sound effect with the real constructor and the closed case-4 controlled descriptor (rate 4, size 20, restored), gives it a handle, and runs the real server routine of the function table at delta 2.0 up to three times, so the real host, getter and (on a kill) destroy run. The new public driver `lua/env-sound-handle.lua` (args `root= out= case=21|22 [scenario=]`) creates its evidence exclusively BEFORE boot, keeps the fixture's report whole in `<out>.case.raw` BEFORE validating any of it, checks a closed grammar (rows and keys in order, every value of its kind and domain), the case identity, that the record is as declared, that the cleanup left it released and that the tracking probe's records can follow one another and agree with the handle and cleanup rows, and judges nothing physical: its derived row only joins what the rows say. The guards are in `tests/env_sound_handle.rs` (scripted engine, real exclusive output; four tests: coherent idle-killed and playing-kept shapes, refusal and engine stop, 23 damaged reports each after its positive, argument/module/foreign-output refusals). Nothing production, no host ABI or schema, no flag, fallback, guard, loader, device or network line, and no existing case, oracle or driver changed.

THE HANDLE IS CONTROLLED TEST STATE, NOT AN ORIGINAL PRODUCT. In this headless process `create_single_system_sound_effect` returns NULL (`sound_system_initialised` is FALSE) and, when a buffer duplication fails, it RELEASES the record, so the original never yields a used record whose buffer is absent. The control instead fills element 0 of the REAL static zero-initialised array `current_system_sound_effects` (owned by `soundman.c`, never freed, its address valid through every real call and through a fatal unwind) as a real record would be filled (one sequenced sample, `used` TRUE, `sound_buffer` NULL, `user_data` NULL) with no COM pointer, vtable, sentinel address, global status override or audio code. It exercises the status getter's and the destroy's bodies for a buffer-absent record, not a backend: a PLAYING buffer needs a device that does not exist here and is NOT manufactured (printed as `env-snd-unsupported`), and the original constructor's handle is not reproduced.

MEMORY OWNERSHIP AND SIDE EFFECTS (for Root to validate). The only memory the control touches beyond the entity it creates is element 0 of that static array: refused before any change if the sound system is initialised or the element is in use; filled after the entity exists; on every path after that, an entity that survived and still holds the record is DETACHED first, a record still in use is released by the real destroy (the row says whether the original or the fixture did), and the element is zeroed, all BEFORE any row is written. The entity itself cannot be put back (the process is the isolation boundary; the end row says `process_isolated=required`). The lightning descriptor numbers and the frame delta are restored and the tracking switch is turned off before the rows on the normal-return path (see the correction above: a fatal unwind does none of that). No pointer or address is printed, but the PRIVATE raw report carries the probe's entity slot, entity index and position words (see the correction above). If the engine stops in a control (an invalid host answer), the engine is poisoned and the process ends with the record still held by the static array and the entity, which is valid memory. The shared seed and floating point state are not touched beyond what the real update routines already do in the closed case 4.

FOR ROOT (nothing run by the author; no actual exit is claimed). Build with the env-observer and env-fixture features (the case needs both). Guards: `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_sound_handle` (plus the existing `env_sound_lifetime`, `env_sound_characterization` and `env_play_resumed_parent` suites, unchanged). Controls, each in a fresh process and fresh verified root: `eech-world eech-campaign/crates/eech-world/lua/env-sound-handle.lua root=<root> out=<new path> case=21` and `... case=22` (optionally `scenario=georgia_retail`). The default fixture and the witness 164 oracle are untouched: no existing case index, row or driver changed, and Root's ordinary checks confirm that. To be OBSERVED, not assumed: whether the real getter answers FALSE and clears the marker, what the host did (an idle expiry and a kill, or not), whether the real destroy released the record, and that the fixture's cleanup left it released. Unavailable prerequisites: a sound device and a playing buffer (with an absent buffer the real getter cannot answer TRUE, so the host's playing, no-kill branch is not reachable by this control), the original constructor's handle, the destroy of an invalid effect through the unlink path (`se_updt.c`, not exercised), the matching-subtype cohort scoping of pause and resume (not used here), the other callers of the getter (`se_snd.c`, `en_snd.c`), the retail-size sample lifetime (the controlled descriptor is used, as in case 4), network, audio.

# D3 non-session resumed-parent playback: the default no-op request is ELIDED and a live request stops by name (candidate, 1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; a focused extraction under Task 51 / PR 111, not whole-D3 closure; after d4634ebd2)

WHAT CHANGED. `soundeff.c play_resumed_parent_sound`, NON-SESSION branch (the campaign's hosted build only; the original game's `#else` still computes the range and calls the entry): the campaign no longer takes the range from the main viewpoint to the parent's position (`get_approx_3d_range`) and no longer calls `play_local_entity_sound (en, vp, range)` when the parent's dispatch entry IS the table's own default no-op. The entry is asked of the real function: `en_snd.c campaign_play_entity_sound_is_default (type)` compares `fn_play_local_entity_sound[type]` with the static `default_play_entity_sound` (an entity type outside the table is not default; no mutable `table[0]`, no SESSION entry, no entity type, headless flag or backend availability is assumed). A parent whose entry is anything else is a live request, which this route no longer supports and does not serve silently: the engine stops with `debug_fatal` "host environment: [env-play:unsupported_live_playback] entity_type=N entity_index=M ..." BEFORE any range is computed or any playback function is called (the engine is then poisoned, as for every fatal error). The compiled sound-effect type's own non-default entry is NOT disabled; its presence in the table is legal, only a live request through this route is unsupported. The session branch, the walk of the sound children, the sub-type match, the host-decided validity/play/relink/count, the order, the timers and the network consequences are unchanged; no host service, flag, oracle, baseline, tolerance or accepted evidence changed; the physical range arithmetic left the campaign route and was not moved to another helper.

CANDIDATE WITNESS (beside the immutable original). `ss_envfx.h` `ENV_FXP_PLAY` (with a range word) becomes `ENV_FXP_ELIDED` (no range argument), and the observer (`eech_env_observer.c`) writes protocol version 2: `env-play-report version=2 ...` (same fields) and `env-play-elided seq t phase ent type session rng` rows, with NO range field (a zero is never printed as a measured range); its own default entry is a cross-check of the production predicate (a disagreement is a `bad_points`). The fixture C is unchanged except its comment: case 19 runs as before and its steps count elided requests; case 20 (the recording function makes the fixed-wing and helicopter entries non-default) now ends at the FIRST request in the engine's fatal handler, so no text comes back. The new driver `lua/env-play-elided-parent.lua` is a SEPARATE file derived from the original's (same arguments and modes, protocol 2): it refuses the original's version 1 report and `env-play-call` rows, requires every fixture step to see the default entry, and for `mode=overload` requires `env_case` to FAIL with the named stop for the fixture's fixed-wing or helicopter type (a returned report, a refusal to run, another failure, another type or a message without type and index fails the driver). `lua/env-play-resumed-parent.lua` (protocol 1, driver sha identity of the 6aec43363 run) and all original raw evidence are untouched and remain the only driver for the 8e90ab42e native.

ORIGINAL EVIDENCE THE DECISION RESTS ON (Root's audit `parent-play-original-actual-native8e-driver6aec-20261101.json` SHA256 `59ac9615…36e9` and the independent review disposition `…disposition-message-20261101-1240.json` SHA256 `2cf4a35d…7145`, reported, not reproduced by the author): the exact 8e90ab42e native with the 6aec43363 driver, one scenario: natural 1200 frames, 140 requests, all default, by entity type 23 (routed vehicle) 106, 14 (helicopter) 32, 10 (fixed wing) 2 (`natural_calls_by_type` 10:2,14:32,23:106), only the sound-effect type 32 non-default in the census; the controlled fixed-wing (entity 88249, type 10) and AI helicopter (entity 90816, type 14) pause/resume, 6 steps, 9 default calls, RNG, viewpoint and children restored, rounding 3072, exception flags 2; the deliberate overload, 9 non-default calls identified as a live consumer, entries restored; all three native/controller 0, 6 input checks 0, 69 payloads and the driver unchanged; the complete unarmed run (5191384 B / 17283 rows) reused. The reviewer found nothing blocking and states that a removal will intentionally change the probe/range diagnostics, to be classified and never recomputed to fake observer conformance.

FOR ROOT (nothing run by the author; no actual exit is claimed). Build the candidate native with the env-observer and env-fixture features and the default features (the observer hooks must vanish; `campaign_play_entity_sound_is_default` is part of the hosted build either way). Guards: `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_play_resumed_parent` (the original driver's tests are unchanged; new `the_elided_candidate_witness_accepts_default_elision_and_refuses_the_originals_protocol_and_a_live_default_claim` and `the_deliberate_overload_must_end_at_the_named_unsupported_live_playback_stop_for_a_target_type`). Controls, each in a fresh process and fresh verified root with the candidate native: `eech-world lua/env-play-elided-parent.lua root=<root> out=<new path> mode=natural seconds=120`, `... mode=fixture`, `... mode=overload`; the unarmed 120-second comparison is the candidate against the reused complete original. EXPECTED (to be confirmed, not assumed): natural requests by type as the original's, none refused; fixture rows equal the original's `before/resumed` states, digests, counts, RNG, rounding and viewpoint/child restoration, with `calls` counting elided requests, and the exception flags allowed to differ because no range is computed (that difference is the removal's, to be reported separately and not normalised); overload: `env_case` FAILS with the stop for type 10 (the first target) and the driver records `overload_stopped`, exit 0; the old driver against the candidate native fails at the report version by name (expected, not an acceptance). Intentional differences from the original raw: version 1 -> 2, `env-play-call` rows with `default` and `range_w` -> `env-play-elided` rows without them, the overload's nine recorded calls -> none readable (the fatal stop poisons the engine; a measured zero after it is not claimed).

BOUNDED LIMITATIONS. The overload cannot show, after the stop, that the recording function received no call (the engine is poisoned): the evidence is the engine's own message and the order of the source, and a candidate that served the request would instead return a report, which the driver refuses. The route no longer reads the parent's position for a default entry, so a parent without that attribute would have stopped in the original's read and now elides; this is a reading, not a measurement. The unsupported stop is a fatal error, not a recoverable refusal and not a host service. Only the measured scenario, parent types and frames are covered: no ship, anti-aircraft or other parent type, no other scenario, no network receive path or other callers beyond what the natural run reached, and nothing about any other playback entry point (unchanged). # D3 non-session resumed-parent characterization: the boot-shape witness correction after the first native boot failures (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; characterization only; driver, test and prose only; after 1fb3a4f7b)

Actual evidence (reported by Root, not reproduced by the author): the first native controls with the exact 8e build and the explicit 1fb3a4f7b driver, natural, fixture and overload, EACH failed (native/controller exit 1) after one public Engine.boot and BEFORE any frame or C fixture (`parent-play-first-boot-failure-8e-1fb-20261101.json`; private child reports under `D:/EECH-Migration-Evidence/parent-play-original-native8e-driver1fb-20261101`; all six full input checks 0, 69 payloads, the driver hash unchanged). The raw first report of each was `env-play-report version=1 armed=1 phase=0 capacity=256 calls_total=0 drained=0 pending=0 dropped=0 bad_points=0 defaults_taken=2 defaults_entries=38 defaults_nondefault=0 defaults_types=- defaults_over=0`; the driver required `defaults_taken` to be exactly 1 and refused it; the case raw is empty and no coverage is claimed. The unarmed 120-second control (tool 54962) was reported still RUNNING and must finish and be reused, never repeated; the first three roots are CONSUMED and Root prepares fresh ones after this fix.

Cause (source-read by the author, not a second public boot): the engine's boot (`eech_engine.c`) runs `brief_initialise_game` and the init screen's `full_initialise_game`, whose `init.c` calls `initialise_entity_system` (`en_init.c`, which calls `initialise_entity_functions`, and so the sound defaults); the same boot then calls `reinitialise_entity_system` (`eech_engine.c` line 273, `en_init.c`), which calls `initialise_entity_functions` again. The observer's defaults hook (`eech_env_observer.c`, ENV_FXP_DEFAULTS) increments `defaults_taken` once per run of the defaults loop. So several C initialisations inside ONE public boot are the original's behaviour and count 2 is the coherent single-boot shape; the old message that equated a count above 1 with a second initialisation was wrong. The C and the native 8e are unchanged and this is not an M6 reload or reinitialisation acceptance.

Correction (`lua/env-play-resumed-parent.lua`): the driver observes the count in the first report after the completed boot, requires it to be at least one (zero is still a probe armed after the defaults or no census) and requires it unchanged in every later report (after the natural frames and after the fixture); a later change is refused as a further initialisation after boot. 2 is not hard-coded as a rule, the raw is kept as written, and the dropped, bad-point, unarmed, defaults-nondefault, dimension, phase and counter checks are as strict as before. `tests/env_play_resumed_parent.rs`: new `the_defaults_census_count_is_observed_after_boot_anchored_and_required_unchanged_afterwards` accepts coherent counts 1, 2 and 3 in natural, fixture and overload (with arbitrary later non-default type numbers) and refuses a first count of 0, an increase (1 to 2, 2 to 3), a decrease and a vanished count in the next report, and an increase across the fixture and the overload; the existing "armed too late" negative (count 0) still holds. There was no existing test that expected an initial count of 2 to fail (the only one used 0), so none was changed. The FNV, domain, selector, C and every other check are unchanged.

For Root (not run by the author): red on the frozen 1fb3a4f7b driver (set `EECH_PARENT_PLAY_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST` to `D:/EECH-Migration-Evidence/pc-1fb3a4f7b/eech-campaign/crates/eech-world/lua/env-play-resumed-parent.lua`): `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_play_resumed_parent the_defaults_census_count_is_observed_after_boot_anchored_and_required_unchanged_afterwards` (count 1 is accepted and count 2 is rejected with "taken 2 times where once is required", the one-count assumption and not a setup incompatibility; the meaningful red is already the actual native boot failure); green with the variable unset: `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_play_resumed_parent`. Still UNMEASURED: every native fixture and natural result, the real targets, feature-off behavior, the full floating-point state and whether later engine activity re-runs the defaults. Not characterization acceptance, removal, Tacview, shipping, Task 51 or D3 closure; Tasks 49 and 50 and the other services stay open; D4/D5 unstarted.

Nothing numerical, rotor, session, production parent-play or range code, host ABI, flag, oracle, baseline or published receipt changed.

(1) Digest join. The driver recomputes the fixture's FNV-1a digest (`env_play_digest` and `env_play_mix`: basis 2166136261, bytes least significant first, multiplier 16777619 modulo 2^32; the words are the number of sound children, the number of matching children, the completeness flag 1 and, per matching child, validity, update-list membership, LIFETIME word then VALID-LIFETIME word, which is NOT the printed order) from the target's counts and the printed `before` and `resumed` words and requires equality with `digest_before` and `digest_resumed`; the restored digest still has to equal the before digest. The multiplication is exact in doubles by two 16-bit limbs (every intermediate below 2^42) and the xor is bitwise. The paused and restored states print no raw words, so their digests stay unjoined (the paused one only a word; the restored one only equal to the before digest). The Rust shapes now build the real digest independently of the driver.

(2) Domains. Integers are canonical decimal text (no leading zero, no `-0`, no sign on zero) and in the range of the C type they come from: `%d` int32_t (seeds, the probe's recorded call count `calls`, the end seeds) and `%u` uint32_t (the report's phase, calls_total, drained, pending, dropped and bad_points; a call row's seq, t, phase, ent, type, session and default; a recorded row's type and the overload's recorded count); the other counts are int32_t and non-negative. Absent, refused, other-child and post-boot non-default shapes still pass.

(3) Override. `EECH_PARENT_PLAY_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST` is read with `var_os`; ONLY an absent variable selects the committed driver, and a present value that is empty, not Unicode, missing or a directory fails. The selection is a pure function (`select_driver`) and its guard never mutates the process environment, so the parallel tests are unaffected.

(4) The failing negative of the overload test is repaired: its helper now derives the default and non-default counts from the list and the dataset is three coherent types still lacking the helicopter's (`11,13,32`); the validator is unchanged and the other negatives of that function are coherent as they stand (they reach their named reasons).

For Root (not run by the author): red on the immutable 8e driver `D:/EECH-Migration-Evidence/pc-8e90ab42e/eech-campaign/crates/eech-world/lua/env-play-resumed-parent.lua` (set `EECH_PARENT_PLAY_DRIVER_UNDER_TEST` to that path; the committed positives are accepted by it, its failure is a damaged shape ACCEPTED): `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_play_resumed_parent the_state_digests_and_the_c_integer_domains_are_joined_to_the_serialized_fields`; green with the variable unset: `cargo test --locked -p eech-world --test env_play_resumed_parent` (all tests, including the override guard `the_driver_override_falls_back_only_when_absent_and_fails_for_every_unreadable_value`, which does not depend on the driver under test). # D3 rotor numerical correction after the 38b200b6c controls: narrowing at the original's float assignments and a run-time APU offset, in env_ref.c only (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; after 38b200b6c)

Actual 38b200b6c gates: the pinned Linux and the native Windows runs each passed the kernel / control tests (3) and the witness tests (12), with complete captures, 1152 variant runs and 0 restoration mismatches each; CI had 6 successful jobs (strict lint and the Wine witness 12 included) and exactly three numerical failures, all unchanged oracle rows: BOTH i686 jobs, flight-model row 1 looping amplification expected `3f2aaaab` got `3f2aaaaa` and row 12 right turbine pitch expected `3f8e147a` got `3f8e147b`; Windows x64 under Wine (compiler `x86_64-w64-mingw32-gcc (GCC) 13-posix`, `__GNUC__` 13.0.0; its package lineage is separate), row 7 APU pitch expected `400ccccc` got `400ccccd`. The retained diagnostics are `rotor-control-CI38-{i686,i686-stack,windows-wine}-…json`. MEASURED in the emitted assembly: on i686 (gcc-12 12.4, x87, `__FLT_EVAL_METHOD__` 2) the kernel computes the slap level as `fdivs` (magnitude / 15.0), `fmuls` (times the looping level) and the bound, subtracts it from the looping level in the x87 register and stores the slap float only after the subtraction, while the unmodified control (collaborator calls between the statements) spills it as a float earlier and matches the oracle; `rrpm / 100.0` flows through `bound` into the `+ 0.6` with no first-assignment float store (the staged control has no marker at that assignment either, so its mismatch there is not evidence against restoring the store). On Wine the saturated branch of `bound (apu_rpm * 0.02, 0.0, 2.0) + 0.2` is the load of the constant `.LC70` = `0x400ccccd` (folded, with its narrowing, under the compiler's round-to-nearest) instead of the guarded run-time `addsd` and `cvtsd2ss` that Linux x86_64 executes (`0x400ccccc`).

The correction, `csrc/env_ref.c` only: (1) `ROTOR_STORE_FLOAT (destination, expression)` stores the unchanged expression into an addressable volatile float and reads it back, used at exactly the assignments those reds depend on: `acc_force`, the first `slap_amp`, and the first assignment of `left_turbine_pitch` and `right_turbine_pitch` (the left one for symmetry with the right, the same source contract); on an SSE target the value is already a float there, so nothing changes. (2) The `0.2` of the APU pitch is read from a function-local volatile double of the same value and type, so the addition and the conversion are run-time operations under the guarded round-toward-zero. No global flag, tolerance, oracle, control, target, guard, parser, `build.rs` or Core change; no per-case value. The U surrogate and the changed S control are not oracles and no complete original-engine assembly is claimed. Known finite scope, UNMEASURED: the other float assignments (the looping level and pitch, the turbine and APU levels, the left level times 1.2) and the saturated `bound (…, 0.0, 1.6) + 0.6` additions are unchanged because no measured red depends on them; the exact-target reds decide. Not run here: every build, test and CI. The changed source makes the earlier dd84 default and authority evidence evidence of the older source; no equivalence, shipping or Tacview claim until measured. D3 and Task 51 stay open, with Tasks 49 and 50 and the remaining services; D4/D5 unstarted.

# D3 rotor witness: ENV-HOST-ROTOR-AUTHORITY-1, a zero-sign test that divided by zero, corrected in Lua and tests only (1 November 2026 UTC; SOURCE ONLY, UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; after a17fe6562)

New actual controls on the frozen dd84 native with the unchanged a17fe6562 witness (`host-rotor-authority-native-dd84-witness-a17-20261101`; all inputs unchanged, 0/0 pre/post): the healthy and the changed control each ran the delegate to its result row (22 rows, 12 calls, all records retained) and then ended with native exit 3221225614 (0xC000008E, floating divide by zero), parent controller 4294967295, the exclusively created authority artifact EMPTY. Review `independent-environment-host-rotor-authority-red-a17fe6562-20261101.json` (SHA256 `7fbd813a…a98c`): `word_of` evaluated `1 / x < 0` for a zero, the first probe call is seq 0 / phase 1 with the rpm word `00000000`, the native host's floating point policy after boot traps the divide in hardware and `pcall` cannot catch it; the masked stand-in in the tests could not see it. The faulting instruction and the control bits were NOT captured; none is claimed. Failed roots stay consumed and their artifacts unchanged. Retained and useful, not repeated: withheld and malformed stopped at `[env-provider:rotor_sound.callback]` / `.answer` as `engine_failure_row` after one request with no result row (native 0), their rotor and sweep sidecars empty but the sound initialisation sidecar 6441 B non-empty (a read-only audit that assumed all three empty failed once and was corrected, no native repeated); the old e544 native completed 12 calls with 0 requests: `rotor_host_capability_missing` (a genuine capability red, native 1).

Correction (`lua/env-rotor-host-witness.lua`, `tests/env_rotor_host_witness.rs` and this wording only): the witness no longer divides anywhere. The sign of a zero is read from its printed form (`-0`), and the halves of a word are taken with an exact power of two instead of `/` and `%`; `+0` stays `00000000`, `-0` stays `80000000`, no zero is canonicalised, and every sent / reference / returned join (children, duplicates, no-write fields, actions, sweep rows), the strict delegate grammar and the exclusive fail-closed I/O are unchanged. Guard: a bounded process-isolated child of the test binary runs the witness (the public-control scenario with an idle rotor at rpm +0, healthy and changed, and a matrix of both zeros, the smallest and largest subnormals, the smallest normal number, the largest finite number, plus and minus one and the 30 / 45 rpm thresholds with their ulp neighbours, plus a delegate that loses a zero sign) with the divide-by-zero exception UNMASKED in the x87 and SSE controls (`ldmxcsr` / `fldcw`, restored and read back), on x86 Linux and Windows; a child killed by the trap, failed or unfinished fails the parent, and a second child that divides by zero on purpose must die of it so the guard cannot pass silently. `EECH_ROTOR_HOST_WITNESS_UNDER_TEST` runs another copy of the witness (the a17fe6562 one) under the same guard for the red. Nothing in the engine, the native exception policy or the retained outputs is changed or masked.

Retained context, with its precise scope: the completed physical default byte conformance and the accepted bounded diagnostic (fixture `excepts` 0x02 to 0x22, an extra 0x20, hex `%x` type, per review `62920b`) make no exact public floating point claim; the current broader Linux gates are still running; the actual Windows kernel / full World library (295 tests, including the full kernel floating point cases, 0) is already retained. Both `0153` roots are CONSUMED; the new `0245` recovery roots are UNUSED (not touched). No host metadata or backend ownership moved.

Group 1, counter wrap: the use count was also the "seen" marker, so after 2^32 reads a seen key re-entered the first-seen branch and appended to the order array, one past its end once the catalogue was full. Now the pure header `csrc/eech_envobs_store.h` keeps "seen" as its own flag, every unbounded counter of the store is saturating (stops at its maximum, raises one latch `saturated`, printed as a new last field of the store row and REFUSED by the driver), and the order append has its own bound check. Normal counts, first-seen order and the digest are unchanged. A small C self-test (`eech-world/tests/csrc/envobs_store_selftest.c`, outside the engine csrc whose every top-level C file is compiled into the engine; run by `tests/env_sound_init_store.rs`) PRESETS
near-limit states (key at 2^32 - 2 reads on a full catalogue, total reads at the maximum, a corrupted distinct count, the latch at its maximum) with canaries past every array: no billions of iterations, no campaign, no new fixture case.

Group 2, evidence files: `out` and the raw sidecar are now created through a new minimal host primitive `host.output_create_new` (`eech-world/src/output.rs`, the OS atomic create-new open) BEFORE booting; literal path inequality, the read-then-truncate check and `"w"`/`"wb"` are gone. Aliases, existing outputs (readable or not), a sidecar that is another name for `out`, a host without the primitive
and any creation error are refused before anything is booted and nothing is truncated; the native text is written, flushed and closed (checked) before validation; nothing derived follows a partial capture. shipping/Tacviews/backend/delivery claim, D4/D5 unstarted. This batch is ORIGINAL code observation only. Nothing is moved: no metadata, backend, per-family, destroy or time behavior and no new host command. The accepted 299 sound driver (SHA256 `7b1f3d491a20…a24e`), cases 0 to 14, closed kernels/witness and baselines are unchanged.

New: a TEST-ONLY sound initialisation probe (`ENV_FXA` hooks in `ss_envfx.h`, placed in `snd_init.c`, `snd_data.c` and the hosted block of `soundeff.c`; store, report and the platform counters in `eech_env_observer.c`, `eech_win32.c` `eech_fopen` and `eech_com_null.c`),
armed by nothing and never reset by arming, in a separate store (no event or record is added to the old streams); one read-only fixture case, group 14 index 15 (previously refused); a NEW driver `lua/env-sound-init.lua` and its guards `tests/env_sound_init.rs`. `eech_engine.c` is untouched. Fixture C changed: Root must build a NEW original observer native.

Source finding that changes the earlier plan: `initialise_application_sound_effects` runs unconditionally and OPENS `audio\sound.hdr` (it needs `sound.hdr` AND `sound.dat`, retried under the installation root's `cohokum\`) to set each descriptor's `size`; only the sample-data loaders are behind the backend result. A descriptor size of 0 therefore does not mean "samples
not loaded" and has several possible causes (the header/data files not found or opened, no header entry for the descriptor, or a matched entry whose length is 0) that the source alone does not separate. Also by source reading: `eech_engine.c boot` runs the sound initialisation while the dedicated flag is still FALSE and the headless `DirectSoundCreate` is the null `E_FAIL` entry point, so the backend is expected to be attempted and to fail at its first call: INFERENCES until the probe measures them (flags and branch, what each DirectSound entry
returned, header/data open attempts and results, header count and matches, catalogue counts and digest, loader calls, the constructor's descriptor reads). The census of references to the descriptor table is in the documentation (source; compiled file vs executable block distinguished; commented and debug-excluded references are not active readers; no part of the playback code is called dormant; runtime liveness not measured); notably `speech.c` keeps only samples with `size > 0`, so a future host metadata table with real sizes would change the speech
stream and must be introduced deliberately.

Feature-OFF scope (checked by me against HEAD with the real staging and the same defines, `__FILE__` prefix-mapped): `eech_win32.c` and `eech_com_null.c` preprocess RAW IDENTICAL; `snd_init.c` differs only in blank lines; `snd_data.c` in blank lines and ONE `__LINE__` integer literal; `soundeff.c` in blank lines and FIVE; nothing unexplained. This is not a whole-DLL identity claim; ordinary
shipping production equivalence stays Root's to prove.

# D3 host-owned sound pause/resume — source prepared (1 November 2026 UTC; UNBUILT natively, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED; nothing ran natively or on retail)

Uncommitted source batch on top of `5e7101d87` (the reviewed original pause/resume characterization; baseline native `33c015557` plus frozen Lua `299bcf05`, 4 SESSION-parent cases, finite scope).
It moves the physical VALIDITY, NO-OP, delay-copy, resume playback-request and update-list relink decisions and the changed counts of ONE matching sound effect out of `soundeff.c`
into the host, as one more public ENVIRONMENT-provider operation, `sound_pause_resume { resume, valid, member, valid_lifetime, delay? }` ->
`{ changed, valid, valid_lifetime, play, relink }`. The legacy children walk and sub type match, the stored fields, HOW the parent plays (the SESSION's default no-op; the
non-session range and per-family branch is the original code, called only when the host asks, and is NOT characterized or moved), the callers and the comms transmission stay Core.

changes only a valid effect, a resume only an invalid one and leaves the valid lifetime, no double relink) before the validity/valid lifetime are written, then the playback
request, then the relink, then the count, in the original order. The host entry has no floating-point work (32-bit words; negative zero and subnormal stale words round-trip).
Files: `ss_hostenv.h/.c`, `soundeff.c`, `env_ref.c/.h`, `env.rs`, `eech-engine-sys`, `eech-engine`, `eech-dc` module, the witness Lua (six more modes) and their tests, plus docs.

# D3 host-owned sound lifetime — the two groups of the source review of 402ba4410 corrected (31 October 2026; source only, UNBUILT natively, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED)

Candidate `402ba441003e362486d3591c2d078f4a9b1d3bbf`; consolidated review `independent-environment-host-sound-lifetime-source-review-402ba4410-20261031.json` (SHA-256 `0b5f4680...f8cb`): ENV-HOST-SOUND-1 (P1) and ENV-HOST-SOUND-2 (P2). One focused uncommitted batch; **changed production C: a NEW immutable native is needed, never a 116 or 402 binary relabelled.** The 402 build/raw receipts and the 18 unused fresh roots are untouched; nothing ran natively.

**1 (P1) floating-point scope.** `env_ref_sound_init` / `env_ref_sound_update` no longer copy floats before `fp_save` or classify/assign them after `fp_leave`: outputs are initialised and published as bits (`memcpy`), floats are loaded after `fp_enter_canonical`, finiteness and sign are classified inside the scope into an integer flag, results are settled, the caller's state is restored and read back, then the staged bits are copied out. Expressions, order and stores unchanged; no global exception masking; no tolerance changed. The existing extended floating-point-state test (every pairing of rounding, precision, sticky flags, denormal modes and arranged status words, answers and complete caller state compared) now includes the refusals decided inside the scope: signalling NaN as lifetime, as delta and as an invalid effect's valid lifetime, a quiet NaN and an infinity.

**2 (P2) witness init modes.** The wrapper forwards every request unchanged while the delegate boots (the campaign's boot asks `sound_lifetime_init` for its looping ambient sounds) and counts the mode's operation apart as `boot_requests`; the alteration, refusal or malformed answer is armed only for the fixture phase (the phase turns at the return of the delegate's `dc.boot`, which is wrapped for the run and restored). `changed_init` therefore meets the case 6 constructor (0.25 in the real constructor state) and `withheld_init` stops at the case 6 `engine_failure` row; the scripted guards model the boot init requests (including the campaign's refusal of a non-zero looping lifetime). `changed` now reconciles the delegate's REAL entity words with the answers it sent: the constructor's lifetime word equals the word after every call (so the host's aging, not only its kill decision, is what the campaign applied) and nobody killed; `healthy` also requires an aged word after the first call; a native that asks the host but ages itself is `ownership-not-shown` (guard added). The delegate's grammar, rows and behavior are unchanged; the authority record gains `boot_requests` and `life_words`.

**WIP actuals (my own; Linux container, offline `--rm` private `/tmp/t`, Docker verified idle first, no Root cache touched; exclusive-new logs `sound-fix402-WIP-20261101-c1` (rustfmt on a private copy, outputs `sound-fix402-fmt-out-20261101-c1`) and `-c2`; every step's exit captured separately):** c2: fmt 0; default `cargo check` of `eech-engine` and `eech-dc --tests` 0 (the corrected C compiles); observer-feature check 0; `env_sound_lifetime` 14 passed (8 earlier witness-related guards extended: boot phase, `boot_requests`, the aged-nokill negative); `eech-world --lib` 264 passed (including the extended FP-state test); `eech-dc --lib` 28; `eech-engine --lib env` 4; clippy `-D warnings` over the three crates 0. The earlier `a4` log (strict error under a misleading exit 0) is retained and untrusted for exit codes. The date prose of the sound-doc heading is corrected to 31 October. `eech_engine.c` untouched (exactly 1 NUL).

**Still the campaign's / still open (unchanged):** the sound database and asset lookup (the descriptor ints are handed over by value), the persistent record, pause/resume validity and update-list relink (40 compiled files), the independent destroy path, network pack/unpack, per-vehicle amplitude/pitch, playback handles and device, the speech cases. **Root next:** pin; NEW native; the old-safe 116 missing-host capability red (`healthy`, case 4: host asked 0 times in the fixture phase); the 11 same-original controls and complete raw comparison; the authority modes; the same reviewer on the changed source; actual review, shipping checks, CI, merges. # D3 host-owned sound lifetime — first candidate extraction, source prepared (31 October 2026; UNBUILT natively, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED)

Clean HEAD `a53e71b365911d49a4df4b51982ef6952296b5e3`; the finite original-domain characterization is closed (native 116ffd5fb, drivers 2a/d4; combined actual review `89c26171...3d2`). **This batch moves the characterized ORIGINAL sound-lifetime decisions into the host** (EECH_WORLD reference), through two new operations of the existing public environment provider; one focused uncommitted batch. Not whole Task 51/D3.

**Moved (hosted EECH_DC; the classic `#else` keeps the original code):** `sound_lifetime_init` = the constructor's sum of the samples' durations, the 40 s / 300 s music cap and the looping case (`soundeff.c`); `sound_lifetime_update` = one call of `update_server` / `update_client`: the aging of a valid timed effect, the server's expiry and kill decision, the client's clamp at zero, and the countdown, zero crossing and next-update unlink of an invalid effect (`se_updt.c`). The reference kernels (`env_ref.c`, `env.rs`) are the original C expressions (same types and order, the engine's `max`/`min`) in the canonical arithmetic environment on staged copies, committed after the caller's FP state is restored and read back. The host keeps NO state.

**Exact data and why by value.** The request carries the sample descriptors' ints (rate, size) for a timed effect; the update request carries the delta (read where the original read it, so the previous/current delta phase is the original's), validity, looping flag, stored lifetime and valid lifetime, and whether a handle is held; a server's expired valid effect with a handle makes the host ask `need_playing` ONCE (nothing changed) and the campaign answers from its sound system. The stored lifetimes stay in the effect's record because pause/resume, the network pack/unpack and the observer read and write them: a host-held copy would diverge. The campaign writes both lifetimes from the CHECKED answer and then does what the host decided in the original order and at the original probe points (`ENV_FXE_*` hooks unchanged): kill, or unlink from the update list and release the handle. Answer checks before any write: finite numbers; only a valid timed effect's lifetime and an invalid effect's valid lifetime may change; an expiry belongs to a valid effect, a kill to the server's expired effect, an unlink to an invalid one; a looping effect's constructor lifetime is +0; a sequence of 0 or more than 16 samples is refused, not truncated. A missing provider, a refusal or a malformed answer stops the engine naming `[env-provider:sound_lifetime_*...]`; no campaign arithmetic remains in the hosted build.

**Data workaround, named.** The campaign still reads the sample database (`application_sound_effects[]`, from the retail `sound.hdr`) and hands the descriptor numbers over: host ownership of the sound metadata and asset lookup is NOT done. **Still the campaign's, explicit (not a quiet D3 deferral):** pause/resume validity and update-list relink (40 compiled files reference them), the independent destroy path, the network pack/unpack/transmission of lifetimes, per-vehicle amplitude/pitch, the playback handles and device, the speech cases, and the dropped constructor diagnostics (a non-positive rate and a sum at the cap were only logged). Cost: every sound-effect update (loops included) is now one provider request: to be measured.

**Tests and witness.** Reference kernels reproduce the measured originals (server 5->3->1->kill, client 5->3->1->0/no kill, sequence 4, caps 40/300, loops 0, invalid 1.5->0.5->0 then unlink, both routines), refuse a bad sequence, and are inside the extended floating-point-state test (caller x87/MXCSR state restored and not leaked; answers independent of it, subnormals included). Boundary guards in `eech-engine` (answer checked before the C side reads it), Lua-bridge guards in `eech-dc` (request by value, exact answer, refusals), and the compact ownership witness `lua/env-sound-host-witness.lua` (modes healthy, changed, changed_init, withheld, malformed, withheld_init, with 5 scripted guards) reusing the lightning witness pattern and the existing control driver. Scenario entrypoints are the existing cases 4..10 and the natural run.

**WIP actuals (my own; Linux container, no network, Docker verified idle first; no Root cache touched; exclusive-new logs `sound-host-WIP-20261101-a1..a5`, `sound-hostfmt-WIP-20261101-b1`):** a1 default `cargo check` of `eech-engine` and `eech-dc --tests` 0 (the changed hosted C compiles); a4: fmt 0, observer-feature check 0 (the hooks compile in the hosted branch), `env_sound_lifetime` 14 passed (9 earlier + 5 witness), `eech-world --lib` 264 passed, `eech-dc --lib` 28, `eech-engine --lib env` 4; a4's clippy printed an unused-import error (a test-only constant, fixed) while its exit tag read 0, so the exit tag of that run is NOT trusted; a5: fmt 0, clippy `-D warnings` over `eech-world`, `eech-dc`, `eech-engine` clean, `eech-dc` sound 2 passed. rustfmt was applied to private copies and only the formatted changed files were copied back. `eech_engine.c` untouched (exactly 1 NUL). **Nothing ran natively or on retail.**

**Root next:** pin; build a NEW immutable candidate native (new C: never relabel 116); run the genuine old116 missing-host capability witness (`healthy` on case 4: the old control completes with the host asked 0 times, `sound-host-capability-missing`); then the candidate comparisons (cases 4..10 against the finite original measurements, the natural run and every legacy raw row, the three primitive controls) and the real `changed`, `changed_init`, `withheld`, `malformed`, `withheld_init` controls; the same source reviewer on this delta, an independent actual review, deterministic reports/Tacview, full 40/all 8 complete byte comparisons, current CI and merges. Whole D3 remaining: the not-moved sound domains above, time, required terrain/spatial/model, full shipping 40 / all 8 complete byte identity, current CI, review, normal merges; D4/D5 unstarted; the six original shipping processes untouched.

Clean pushed `260354e16cf3d604679914b3e7a6cae667441123`; review `independent-environment-original-sound-source-review-260354e16-20261031.json` (SHA-256 `7bd0578e...c256`): all four prior counter forms closed, the P1 native fix reused, exactly ONE related P2 (`ENV-SOUND-ORIGINAL-2-JOURNAL-GUARD`): the positive `sound_playing` form I added in the previous batch was not a coherent capture. It gave playing-handle expiries at updates 3, 7, 9 although the probe samples only entries and exits after the third entry and stores EVERY expiry point (so 4, 5, 6 and 8 would have been recorded), and every record had `handle=0` although a playing/idle expiry needs a handle. **Only Lua, guards and docs changed; no C, Rust production, fixture, observer or build input.** The 942 native is reused with the new pinned Lua overrides; this is not a new binary.

**Fixed.** Stand-in: `sound_playing` is now the complete consecutive journal (playing expiry at every update 3 to 9, handle from update 3; created state handle 0 as the real constructor leaves it). Natural validator: in the supported journal (every reading record valid and non-looping, as `create_lightning_effects` passes TRUE/FALSE; a record outside it is refused, not recomputed) once a playing-handle expiry is observed at update u the next record is the entry of u+1 (while entries are stored) or the expiry of u+1, an entry after it is followed by the expiry of its own update, and a chain may not end between them; an expiry with no handle shows none, with a handle shows one, sees the handle of its own update's entry, and a handle stays once shown; the creation record has no handle. The primitive control got the handle-truth rule. Preserved: the sampling gap before the FIRST expiry (`sound_long`), one-update early kills, alive lower bounds, the other positive forms. **Limitation, stated not computed:** these rules hold only for the valid, non-looping, positive-delta journal; an unrecorded change of `valid`, time or external state would make "every later update reaches an expiry" unprovable, so such records are refused. No handle is ever created by the characterization; the stand-ins are canned protocol checks, not backend availability or playing coverage.

**New guards:** the playing journal with the gap (`sound_playing_gap`, which is the old form with the handle shown), an entry after a playing expiry with no expiry of its update (`sound_playing_skip`), a playing expiry that shows no handle, an expiry that sees another handle than its entry, and (control) an expiry with no handle that shows one. Two of my own damaged-report expectations (a point changed to 4) now meet the handle rule before the transition rule; they were changed to a kill turned into a client entry (still "cannot follow point 2").

**Actual old-260 acceptances (retained):** `sound-red-actual-260-journal-20261031-r3.log` (one container, a copy of this tree with the four 260354e16 files restored and two refusal tests added; `/src` untouched): the control accepted an expiry-with-no-handle whose record shows a handle, and the natural validator accepted its own positive journal (3, 7, 9 with 4, 5, 6, 8 missing and handle 0 everywhere). Earlier reds and greens retained untouched (`...c9... r1`, `...942... r2`, b1/b2/c1).

**WIP actuals (my own; Linux container, no network, Docker verified idle first; world tests only, no C compile; nothing of Root's or the in-use caches touched):** first run failed (rustfmt on one tuple and the two expectations above): its raw log was OVERWRITTEN by my rerun under the same name, so only a labelled excerpt remains (`sound-journal-WIP-20261031-d1-first-run-excerpt.txt`); the green rerun is `sound-journal-WIP-20261031-d2-green.log`: `cargo fmt --all --check` 0; `env_sound_lifetime` 5 passed; `host_callbacks` 45 passed; `env_lightning_queue` 18 passed; clippy `-D warnings` 0. `eech_engine.c` still exactly 1 NUL.

**Unchanged and open:** the playing/idle-handle branches stay unsupported (no device, nothing faked); `valid=false`, looping, audible, network and player delivery uncovered; the sound responsibility is not moved. **Root:** pin and freeze the Lua-only delta, the same reviewer on the new delta, then the four retail original sound characterizations and the comparison of every legacy raw row before any HOST move. **Fixed (bookkeeping coherence, not a lifetime/amplitude/outcome oracle).** Primitive control (at most two calls, every entry stored): an expiry or a kill must carry the CURRENT entry count, and a kill the update of the expiry straight before it. Natural journal: until all three mandatory entries have been stored an expiry/kill carries exactly the current stored-entry count (it may not run ahead across an unstored entry); only after the third entry may it carry a later, only-counted update, never below the update before it; a kill carries its expiry's update. Natural: an early kill with one stored entry whose expiry/kill jump to update 4 with the total raised to 8 (`sound_early_jump`); a kill in update 9 after an expiry in update 7 with the total raised to 16 (`sound_kill_other_update`).

**Actual old-942 acceptances (retained):** `sound-red-actual-942-lua-20261031-r2.log` (one container, a copy of this tree with the four 9429beefb files restored and only the new forms added as refusal tests; `/src` untouched). # D3 original sound probe — CORRECTION batch for the independent review of c9c1b0a1b (31 October 2026; native UNBUILT, UNRUN, UNREVIEWED)

Clean pushed `c9c1b0a1bed1b3e4bbfc31e87b37e18c2b446489`; review `independent-environment-original-sound-source-review-c9c1b0a1b-20261031.json` (SHA-256 `98ef37b6...ad3a`): one P1, one P2, one scope qualification. Source only; no sound production move, no host API, no new planner/backlog/framework.

**P1 (fixture use after the first kill) fixed.** Control 1 no longer calls the real server update a second time unconditionally. The sample data sets the lifetime and it is never lengthened; the first real call may kill the entity (freeing the sound data and clearing the slot's type and data). After each real call only the stable entity-slot type and data are read; a terminal entity is not updated again; the end row says `calls=`, `second_skipped=`, `etype_after`, `data_after`. Every row that can fail to fit is written before the entity exists and before the delta or tracking is touched, so no return leaves either changed. The constructor's prerequisite is explicit: no free entity slot (`get_free_entity` would return NULL and the constructor would dereference it) is a printed refusal. The original C update/creation behaviour is unchanged. No old use-after-free was executed: the source finding is the proof. A legitimate one-update early kill is accepted by the driver and guarded (`early_kill_report`), alongside the two-update form.

**P2 (cohort coherence) fixed in both validators, existing grammar and helpers.** Operation pairs: an expiry (no/idle handle) must be followed by its kill, a client entry by its exit. Update counters: the j-th stored entry of an update is update j (only the first three are stored, declared scope kept), exit/expiry/kill never go below the update they end, a per-entity count above the probe's total is refused, and the total must equal the entities' counts when every one is exact (a killed entity's count, or a count below three) and is otherwise a lower bound. Control end facts agree with the records and the calls (`calls` = entries = probe updates; `second_skipped` only after a kill; a chain with no kill leaves the live sound entity). The unaccepted healthy client stand-in is corrected (a client chain has no kill, so `etype_after=25 data_after=1`); this is not a baseline or oracle change. Natural creations are matched to the observer's own ordered `sound_begin`/`sound_end` pair (same ordinal, same frame, entity = the index the observer saw next to be given), followers' `n` match their creation. Source substantiation, not a call-count hypothesis: `create_client_server_sound_effect_entity` has only assertions and a message after the constructor, no skip branch; `se_creat.c` takes one `get_free_entity` (DONT_CARE = the first free slot); heap exhaustion would fault, not skip. Tracking loss (more than eight creations) is now counted (`untracked=` in the report) and classified (`sound_untracked` row, `untracked_creations`, `lifetimes_complete=false` in the summary); the cap is not raised and untracked lifetimes are not claimed. The playing-handle repeat (`4` after `4`, `3` after `4`) is allowed in the chain grammar.

**Actual REDs of the c9c1b0a1b validators (retained):** log `sound-red-actual-old-validators-20261031-r1.log` (one container, a copy of this tree with the four c9c1 files restored and only old-grammar tests added; `/src` untouched). Control driver: stranded expiry with kill removed and stored/kills reduced, a first entry at update 99 then 2, and the healthy client stand-in's contradiction were ACCEPTED (3 of 3). Natural driver: stranded expiry, two `n=1` creations, and a one-followed-of-two creation run reported as a plain success with no loss classification were ACCEPTED (3 of 3). Field-only new-format rejections are not claimed as old reds. These reds precede the fix and are not a claim about the original sound behaviour.

**WIP actuals (my own, Linux container, no network, Docker verified idle first, logs `sound-fix-WIP-20261031-b1.log` and `-b2.log`):** b1 found one stale expectation in my new damaged-report list (fixed) and rustfmt differences in three new test lines (fixed). b2: `cargo fmt --all --check` 0; `cargo check` `eech-engine`/`eech-dc` default features 0 and with `env-observer` 0 (the C compiles); `env_sound_lifetime` 5 passed; `host_callbacks` 45 passed; `env_lightning_queue` 18 passed; clippy `-D warnings` 0. `git diff --check` clean apart from benign CRLF warnings; `eech_engine.c` still exactly 1 NUL. Earlier a1/a2/a3 logs retained. **Not run by me:** a native on the new C, the feature-off token check, any retail/real boot, the natural run, the three controls, Windows, CI. Every number in the stand-ins is canned.

**Scope qualification (docs/STATUS corrected, supersedes "feature off, no original body token changes" for six files).** Only the two production hook bodies (`soundeff.c create_local_sound_effect_entity`, `se_updt.c update_server`/`update_client`) retain their executable tokens when `ENV_FXE` expands to nothing; that is a source reading, not an object-file, numeric, runtime or shipping equivalence, and the added include, macro definitions and `__LINE__` shifts are not claimed away. `eech_env_fixture.c` is a test entry point that changes even with the observer off (`ENV_GROUP_LAST` 13 to 14; group 14 dispatches to a diagnostic refusal there). `eech_env_observer.c` is test-only. The Lua entry point has new optional code, which is not C preprocessor identity. These C changes (fixture skip, untracked counter, new report/end-row fields) need a NEW native build; no c9 binary is reused for them.

**Root, next:** pin this delta; same reviewer on the delta; then the new native, the canonical checks, ONE fresh fixed-Lebanon 120 s run with `queue=on sound=on` and the three controls in fresh roots, every legacy raw row compared (typed differences, no normalisation), before any HOST sound move. job-object framework is claimed or added). The earlier controller receipt's "ten checks" boolean was vacuously true with no PRE stages (Root preserves it with an audit and requires exactly 5 pre + 5 post): no ten-check or
five-mode pass is claimed here. Other actuals (Root): corrected 14192 default 95-row cases exact to the `b2e` originals on all 3 roots (receipt `environment-sector-fixed-14192c6e2-comparison-20261031.json`); Windows libs 78/22/251

* **Windows runtime memory-safety defect of the Step 6 registry, repaired in source (`f4700bbab` run; candidate, NOT accepted; no native validation of the repair):** the Root's first native run of the six shipping `object-report-case` roots on `f4700bbab` actually terminated all six at native -1073741819 (0xC0000005) with only the boot row in each trace (`root-r3-f470-six-report-crashes.json`, `root-r3-f470-structure-crash-disassembly.log`); none establishes report influence, withholding, generic-host authority or a refusal. Cause, verified in source: `campaign_object_structures` walked the entity heap to the compile-time ceiling `MAX_NUM_ENTITIES` (524,288) while the run allocated `number_of_entities` slots (125,000 or 135,000) and `get_local_entity_ptr` is unchecked. Every slot index of the registry (enumeration, identity map allocation, lookup, identification, release) is now bounded by the capacity actually allocated, and a heap that changes size under live identities is refused; nothing else changed (kinds, order, identities, lifecycle, report semantics, handlers, allocation, numbering, physics). The fake heap that hid it is replaced by one allocated EXACTLY the run's capacity below the ceiling with a counting accessor: against the `f4700bbab` registry the capacity checks fail (19 of 82, at five runtime capacities), against the fix 82 pass under ASan/UBSan; 23 mutants caught; real-tree compile, workspace and fixture `cargo test`, fmt and syntax pass. The Root rebuilds a clean source and reruns the six roots; the repaired source is not claimed to work natively until then. See `eech-campaign/docs/m5-combat-extraction.md` Step 6. D2 remains active, whole D2 not complete.
* **Guard order of the structure registry after the focused review of the heap-capacity fix (`30fbdfcbc`; one P2, one P3; candidate, NOT accepted; no Windows or native run of this source):** the heap, the map and the slot are validated first in `campaign_object_host_id`, `campaign_object_identify` (before the cache, the type read and the host question), the report resolution (before any record is followed; a rostered target's slot before it is read) and the enumeration, so a heap that is not the registry's is refused by name without returning an identity, asking the host or applying anything. 94 checks (exact-capacity heap, instrumented entity access, pointer at the capacity, cached and fresh identity and reports after a capacity change, both stale slots, restore), 26 mutants caught, 12 failures against `30fbdfcbc`. Evidence wording corrected (an out-of-bounds read faulted, not "the first"; the old map was sized for the ceiling, not "2 MiB excess"; the old red was guarded-access instrumentation, not an ASan reproduction). See `eech-campaign/docs/m5-combat-extraction.md` Step 6. Whole D2 is not complete.
